# CLEAN1A — code inventory, 19 October 2026

Inventory only. The [owner brief](../OWNER_BRIEF.md) was read first. Source baseline: main checkout on `fable/label-correction` at `4e95489d9`; the `main` ref is `7f50b69fb`. Inventory checkout: `cleanup/20261019` at `14fc9efe7`. No scripts, tests, models, network operations, checkouts, commits or cleanup actions were run. This run wrote only `INVENTORY_CODE.md` and `code.json`.

Measured through 2026-10-19 19:04:15 UTC: **403 main files** (402 tracked plus untracked `greekocr/labelfix/spellink.py`), **459 added paths against the tracked baseline**, **1,713 file/branch occurrences covering 861 distinct paths**, and **1,215 directory occurrences**. Main has **73,588 newline characters under greekocr/** and **25,060 under scripts/**, including resources/configuration; these are not logical SLOC.

`spellink.py` also has tracked branch versions on seven named refs. Thus 458 added paths are absent from main’s observed filesystem, while 459 are absent from its tracked baseline. Common-path variants are not repeated as file rows; JSON includes their exact newline counts and directory totals, and committed `diff --stat` results against `fable/label-correction`.

## Classification and measurement rules

| Classification | Owner scope |
| --- | --- |
| `A.i` | Development of OCR/segmenter: slow explicit pixel detector and its crop primitives |
| `A.ii` | Development of OCR/segmenter: next-generation R8 revision of the fast stack |
| `A.iii` | Development of OCR/segmenter: small Paddle detector plus corrections (R7 lineage) |
| `A` | OCR development outside the three segmenter slots (recognition, scoring, donor conversion, synthetic factory) |
| `B.a` | Training-data creation: Google/page OCR, readers and source acquisition; whole readers namespace assigned here as requested |
| `B.b` | Training-data creation: correction/filtering tools using the segmenter/crop evidence |
| `B.c` | Training-data creation: exact organising pipeline; historical collection builders are identified separately |
| `B.d` | Training-data creation: revision, adjudication, review sheets/artifacts and publication preparation |
| `shared infrastructure` | deploy, ops, verify, types, cli, _vendor and generated infrastructure |
| `obsolete` | Only where evidence establishes obsolescence; none inferred from filenames or zero import counts |
| `unsure` | Purpose/classification cannot be established from inspected source and documentation |

Composite directory classifications list their members’ slots. Purposes come from module docstrings in the first 24 lines, matching README entries and package documentation. **212 file occurrences lack sufficient purpose evidence** and explicitly say unknown. Lifecycle is **current / parked / superseded / unsure**, with the source key below; current describes documented support, not scientific acceptance or deployment. Unknown lifecycle remains unknown. No zero-reference module is declared obsolete.

Line counts are newline characters including comments, blanks and data. Zero does not mean empty. Physical file SHA-256s bind the inspected source; ref-only rehearsal contents were read with `git show` and have null SHA-256s. Generated bytecode/Rust target files are excluded from source totals; generated directories have null line counts. No global filesystem freeze was imposed.

Import/test evidence is static: qualified and relative imports, multiline from lists, and bare imports with an existing sibling module. A `from package import child` can reference both the initializer and an existing child module. Physical scans cover `greekocr`, `scripts`, `tests`, plus absolute GreekOCR/scripts imports in `prototypes` and `seed`. Rehearsal refs use actual Git source/test imports; bare imports in common-path modules were not separately rescanned there. Dynamic loading, arbitrary sys.path, shell/CLI/subprocess use, Rust mod/use, resources and transitive coverage are unresolved. **No match is not proof of dead code.** JSON records bare-import candidates separately.

## The three segmenters

The owner’s three slots do **not** identify three independent installed packages. The [dated generation record](/Users/foivoskarounos-zamparloukos/Projects/greek-ocr-toolkit-ff-ink/docs/research/round8/MODEL_GENERATIONS.md) calls the original pixel model generation 1, small Paddle plus heuristics generation 2, and its R8 revision generation 3. A.ii “next-generation” and A.iii “built on the small detector” therefore overlap in their current code path.

| Classification | Concrete entry points and implementation | Latest located branch/code | State and evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A.i | `greekocr.pages.process_page`, `process_book`, `pages.lines.segment_page`; CLI `greekocr pages`. `pages.pixels` → discovery/recovery and shared geometry in `pages.lines` → ownership/font guides/boundaries → delivery. Crop primitives remain in `greekocr.glyphs`. | Present in main and `astra/page-lines-4`. Pixel-first introduction `f405fe38b` (9 Oct), Q3 relocation `bb06ee4ec`, maintained Q4 snapshot `76ad6fb4f`. Last page/glyph code-touch on lines4: `3f75344e6` (10 Oct, optional torch experiment). | **current** supported API and slow reference, with known errors. Orientation is parked; experimental torch paths are not the default detector. See PAGES_GUIDE and MODEL_GENERATIONS. |
| A.ii | `prototypes.fast_lines.stack.Stack`; CLI `python -m prototypes.fast_lines.stack IMAGE --output FILE`. `stack/pipeline.py` orchestrates shared geometry, ink, type, recovery and reconciliation. | Last runtime code-touch **`046bd52dd` (15 Oct)** is on `astra/ff-ink`, `astra/ff-merged` and `astra/ff-r8-stack`. The ff-ink/ff-merged prototype diff stat is empty; ff-merged HEAD `cae93fb49` additionally includes planning/audit docs. | **unsure as an adopted release; current research revision**. Final runtime audit leaves architecture, physical method, output and speed goals partial and says independent audit acceptance is pending. |
| A.iii | Historical R7 stack at the same `Stack` path, based on PaddleOCR PP-OCRv6 **small**. Raw base is `shared.detector.Detector` plus DB postprocessing, CPU ONNX. | `astra/page-lines-4` holds the earlier Q8 lineage (`b5199adcc`, 10 Oct); ff-ink/ff-merged contain the later stack. | **superseded as the R7 revision**, with this architecture still live in R8. Not a separate obsolete package. |

Read the [implementation map](/Users/foivoskarounos-zamparloukos/Projects/greek-ocr-toolkit-ff-merged/docs/LINE_SEGMENTATION.md), [method](/Users/foivoskarounos-zamparloukos/Projects/greek-ocr-toolkit-ff-merged/docs/LINE_SEGMENTATION_METHOD.md), [checked audit](/Users/foivoskarounos-zamparloukos/Projects/greek-ocr-toolkit-ff-merged/docs/research/round8/AUDIT.md) and [stack README](/Users/foivoskarounos-zamparloukos/Projects/greek-ocr-toolkit-ff-ink/prototypes/fast_lines/stack/README.md). Historical timing/physical results were read, not reproduced. Defaults in that audit are Python, `bend=False`, `final_export=False`; FONT is parked and Rust qualification is separate.

If A.iii means a narrower component, the evidence supports these candidates:

- **Full stack:** `Stack` is the strongest match; its README explicitly describes one file-to-lines entry using a warm small-Paddle session.
- **Raw neural base:** `shared.detector.Detector` supplies maps/localisations, not the full correction pipeline.
- **Q8 correction:** `layout_barriers.fast.run(gray, paddle_items)` is the conditional printed-rule separator, not a complete detector/recovery solution.
- **Q7 composition:** `python -m prototypes.fast_lines.run compose` is the earlier experiment graph; docs distinguish it from current `Stack`.

The requested `docs/PAGE_LINES_ALGORITHM.md`, `PAGE_LINE_SEGMENTS.md`, `GLYPHS_PLAN.md`, `GLYPHS_CALIBRATION.md`, crop/page/warmstart sources and ff-ink generation/method/audit docs were inspected. The box-guided `glyphs.recut.fix_page` / `glyphs.page_lines` lineage remains for compatibility. Seven old glyph page-stage names alias maintained `pages` modules; they are not seven duplicate algorithms. `greekocr.warmstart` (42 main lines) handles donor conversion/preprocessing/logit parity: **A OCR development**, not a segmenter.

**Requested directories:** main’s `greekocr/lines/`, `scripts/lines/` and `docs/lines/` exist but are **empty**, verified with directory listings and hidden/tracked file inventories. No all-ref path log entries were found for `greekocr/lines` or `docs/lines`. The other named worktrees lack those directories. No implementation was inferred from an empty directory name.

The organising B.c sequence is: bind every raw row/provenance → inventory and expected scripts → content gate/shared raw-text length rule → segmentation screen → schedule all relevant SF7/RU1/N1/dash/content evidence → validate/compose raw-parent edits → re-inventory and recheck newly introduced candidates → final eligibility/codec/CTC/split checks → holdout protection/deduplication → immutable export generation/publication receipt. Holds/drops retain evidence and remain outside admitted training exports; missing sparse corrective output is not a no-change verdict.

**Version drift:** README/pipeline.mmd still label segmentation 0.5, the policy’s leading comment says 0.8, and `tool_versions.json` records **0.9-s4**. Its later checkpoints include row-frame boxes, crop-only gate, rotation, curved baselines and small-component handling. Content gate 0.3 is named in the pipeline docs. These disagreements were recorded without changing source or documentation.

**Build completeness:** the observed `scripts/harvest/filter_runtime` on seg-census contains only `.gitignore`. `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `src/main.rs` and generated target directories exist in `sol/recut-v1` as untracked inputs. A self-contained seg-census Rust build is not established.

B.d consumes saved evidence. The standard is crop plus Google text, the last two tool versions, owner outer/tool inner frames, stable IDs, notes and editable Printed fields. Docs describe claude.ai artifact publication with `db` + `user`, no writes on load, note-collection dump before republish and a mark-reload check afterwards. Blind decks separate public crops/viewer from private keys/provenance. No sheet or remote database was opened, created, published or changed here.

The review branch’s `greekocr/recogniser` is **A OCR development**: the delegating API is required for new OCR tools, while pinned callers retain original imports until recertification. It is not another segmenter or evidence that old inference modules can be deleted.

## Tests

**No tests were run or collected.** Main has **70 pytest-named files**, plus `tests/conftest.py` and `tests/fixtures/harvest_fix1/fetch_selection.py`. Counts are static filenames, not test cases, passes or coverage percentages. Package counts overlap because a test can import several packages.

The main [pyproject.toml](/Users/foivoskarounos-zamparloukos/Projects/greek-ocr-toolkit/pyproject.toml) sets `testpaths = ["tests"]` and `addopts = "-ra --strict-markers"`, with optional test dependencies. README’s command is **`.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q`**. CLI registration is `greekocr = "greekocr.cli:main"`; the Docling entry points to `greekocr.deploy.docling_plugin.docling_plugin`.

There are **41 pytest-named fast-stack files** under ff-ink’s `prototypes/fast_lines`, including stack, geometry, type, ink, atlas and Rust-field tests. They are outside default testpaths. The root prototype README documents PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE plus explicit pytest paths; its older prototypes/fast_lines/tests command does not include every later test directory. The [test guide](/Users/foivoskarounos-zamparloukos/Projects/greek-ocr-toolkit-ff-ink/docs/LINE_SEGMENTATION_TESTS.md) explains current boundaries. Historic pass counts were not promoted as validation by this run.

## Scope and branch index

All **22 named refs** were inspected: **20 worktrees**, **two rehearsal refs** read with Git without checkout/extraction. Funnel wildcard expands to gr/ia/intl, r2 wildcard to cards/prep. Zero branch-only greekocr/scripts paths does not mean no work: a branch may change common paths, docs, collector code or data outside these roots. JSON preserves each committed diff stat against fable/label-correction and observed modified/untracked source status.

## Remaining unknowns

Static source classification/reference measurements cover the stated roots and refs. Many historical/experimental helpers lack an explicit lifecycle ruling, so their state remains unsure. This inventory does not establish safe removal, remote census/release status, live sheet/database state, native crop correctness, private held-out generalisation, installed model readiness or any test pass. Version-label drift and the missing seg-census Rust sources remain concrete follow-up findings. No other inventory run’s files were edited; no commit was made.
